Chapter 432: Taking Advantage of the Situation

Du Changsheng was actually slightly surprised that this person knew Mr. Ji. This man had probably mistaken him for a martial arts master, since the surname Ji, although rare, was not entirely unique.

Regardless, Du Changsheng’s face remained expressionless. He simply nodded slightly and smiled.

“This Heavenly Master lives in a secluded alley in Chunhui Prefecture. Your entry into my courtyard constitutes trespassing. Considering that you were unaware of this and are merely a mortal, you may leave on your own.”

Du Changsheng’s words were polite, but he secretly made a couple of preparations. He had already pinched a talisman in his sleeve; his spiritual energy was much denser than before, and his body had grown stronger, but the quantity of his spiritual energy was still too weak.

After all, forging an alchemy furnace to build a golden bridge between the heaven and earth within the body’s inner world was no simple task. Even though he obtained the true method from Mr. Ji back then, Du Changsheng was not entirely confident that he could succeed before he died of old age.

Therefore, let alone one Du Changsheng, even ten Du Changshengs probably couldn’t defeat a skilled martial arts practitioner who excelled in combat, especially one with such skill in qinggong that they could appear silently right behind him.

With Du Changsheng’s current abilities, although he had some supernatural powers and techniques, they were still far from being effective means of combat. Fortunately, he was quite skilled in creating talismans, which he could use to assist him. In addition, most martial arts masters had limited knowledge of mystical arts, so he still had a good chance of winning in a real fight.

Du Changsheng was well-versed in the principle of underestimating his opponent strategically while taking him seriously tactically.

However, after his initial surprise, the martial artist in front of him did not immediately retreat. Instead, he scrutinized him with a strange look, a gaze that made even the worldly-wise Du Changsheng feel slightly uneasy.

Bai Qi narrowed his eyes, looking Du Changsheng up and down. Judging from his aura, his vitality and qi were still vigorous, so he was not an ordinary old man. His spiritual energy was also quite abundant, but his spiritual energy was shallow and his divine light was dim, so his cultivation level was definitely not very high.

‘Could someone like this be Mr. Ji’s apprentice? Hu Yun is more reliable than him; at least he doesn’t seem so jaded.’

Suppressing his unease, Du Changsheng walked slowly to the small table, sat down on the futon, took a sip of tea, and then squinted at Bai Qi.

“What, not leaving? Well, you came here quietly, you must have a purpose. Judging from the timing of your arrival and your lie about being the God of Chunmu River, it seems you are related to that Li Jinlai. Should I have him tell me about it?”

Avoiding conflict whenever possible was Du Changsheng’s philosophy of self-preservation. Since the visitor was not leaving, it was best to find out his purpose. Whether it was for money or business, it was all negotiable; fighting was the last resort.

“Haha, it does seem plausible, but the more I look at you, the less I believe you could be Mr. Ji’s apprentice.”

Bai Qi smiled calmly and spoke, stepping into the room instead of retreating. However, he did not leap or walk slowly, but rather moved into the room as if he were not subject to gravity, landing silently.

Then Bai Qi reached out and beckoned, and Du Changsheng felt a numbness in his left arm inside his sleeve. The talisman in his hand had flown out on its own and landed in Bai Qi’s hand.

‘A master of innate cultivation retrieving objects from afar? No! This is the supernatural technique of manipulating objects!’1

“Gulp…”

Du Changsheng swallowed a mouthful of tea, his body already stiffening considerably. His eyes were fixed on the teacup, not daring to look at Bai Qi.

“The talisman looks exquisite, but unfortunately it’s not very powerful, and your cultivation is still so shallow…”

At this point, Bai Qi suddenly changed the subject and spoke with a smile.

“The Hermit Immortal Ji Yuan? I’m surprised you came up with that title. Mr. Ji wouldn’t be so high-profile, but since you can name him, you must have met him before.”

“Who…who are you?”

Du Changsheng could no longer maintain his composure and appeared somewhat flustered. Even a fool would realize that the person in front of him was no wandering martial arts practitioner, but an individual with profound skills, the nature of which he simply couldn’t discern.

“Who am I? I already said it when I arrived. I am Bai, the River God of Chunmu River.”

Du Changsheng trembled slightly, quickly put down his teacup, stood up from the futon, and respectfully bowed with his hands clasped.

“I, Du Changsheng, pay my respects to the Lord River God!”

Regardless of whether or not he was the real River God, his cultivation was unfathomable to Du Changsheng, and he did not dare act arrogant again.

Just then, the boy who had seen Li Jinlai out returned and saw Du Changsheng respectfully bowing to a stranger who had somehow entered in the intervening moments.

“Master… what’s wrong?”

“Disciple, this is the River God of Chunmu River. Hurry up and pay your respects to the River God!”

Du Changsheng scolded him, and the boy quickly bowed and greeted.

“Wang Xiao pays his respects to the Lord River God!”

Bai Qi clearly didn’t care whether the two bowed or not. He just glanced at the boy, then waved his hand, and a layer of mist enveloped the entire courtyard.

Then a feeling of weightlessness rose around Du Changsheng and Wang Xiao. Their bodies twisted a few times, and they almost fell. After regaining their balance, they realized that they had been lifted off the ground.

Du Changsheng’s expression changed slightly, and he inwardly panicked, but he at least maintained a calm demeanor. The boy Wang Xiao, on the other hand, grew panicked both in appearance and in his heart.

“Master!”

“Calm down!”

Du Changsheng comforted his disciple, in a way also comforting himself. Then, he carefully tried to stabilize himself, and turned to Bai Qi, who had also lifted himself into the mist with a wave of his sleeve.

“I hope the Lord River God will forgive me. I, Du Changsheng, have not done anything immoral. I gave Li Jinlai the talisman, but it would never have harmed the aquatic creatures in the river. Lord River God, please understand! By the way, does the Lord River God know Mr. Ji? I do know Mr. Ji. The fundamental techniques I learned were taught by him!”

A thin wisp of mist, like a hazy breeze, swept the three of them into the air in an instant, soaring hundreds of feet into the sky. Even though the fog obscured their vision, it only intensified Du Changsheng and Wang Xiao’s fear; if they fell, they would be smashed to pieces.

“Don’t be afraid, I’m taking you to see your master now.”

Bai Qi even had the leisure to make a joke, which made Du Changsheng pause for a moment in surprise.

“My master?”

Wang Xiao also asked in a daze, “Grandmaster?”

Du Changsheng immediately realized what was going on, and, connecting it with Bai Qi’s earlier reaction, an answer became obvious.

‘Is Mr. Ji here?’

The fog-filled wind flew over the capital of Chunhui Prefecture for a a long stretch of distance, then slowly floated over the Chunmu River. It drifted diagonally into the winding, mountain-side section of the river, far from the city, before finally beginning to descend.

As they descended, the fog gradually dissipated, rapidly clearing the visibility around Du Changsheng and Wang Xiao, especially the ground under their feet. They were flying steadily at high altitude when they suddenly began to plummet rapidly.

“Aaaaah…”

“Master!”

The instinctive fear of heights caused both of them to scream in fright.

With two loud thuds, the two landed one after the other on a small painted boat on the river.

Ji Yuan was sitting at the bow of the boat. The two people who had fallen onto the deck were clinging to the gunwale, their heads down. Ji Yuan could easily guess that they had their eyes tightly closed.

“What is going on, River God Bai?”

Ji Yuan asked Bai Qi, slightly puzzled. The latter had just landed lightly and stood at the bow of the small boat.

Bai Qi first bowed to Ji Yuan, then pointed at Du Changsheng and smiled.

“Sir, this is the individual who gave Li Jinlai the talisman, but he didn’t use it for anything nefarious. However, that’s not the point. The point is that he claims to be your disciple.”

“Hmm?”

Ji Yuan exclaimed in surprise.

“My disciple?”

If he truly were to tally up all of his experiences, only Lu Shanjun could be considered Ji Yuan’s true disciple. The Old Turtle could barely be considered a half-disciple, and Bai Ruo, the White Deer Girl, even less so. Hu Yun didn’t qualify at all. The Taoists from Cloud Mountain Temple could be considered to have inherited his Taoist lineage, but the man before him was definitely not one of them.

“Hmm, this man seems kind of familiar…”

Ji Yuan looked at Du Changsheng more carefully. The his supernatural eyes revealed the man’s aura; he did indeed look somewhat familiar.

Du Changsheng, who had been feeling like he was about to be thrown to his death, was startled by Ji Yuan’s words, and looked up in the direction of the voice.

In front of Bai Qi, he saw a small table set at the bow of the small boat set with pastries and tea. Opposite the table, at the tip of the bow, a man in a long white robe with a jade hairpin in his hair was calmly looking in their direction. His pale eyes were like the moon reflected in an ancient well.

Du Changsheng turned around and knelt down as if performing acrobatics for the circus, and bowed his head deeply in worship.

“Sir! Mr. Ji! Master~!”

“Thump thump thump…”

His head slammed against the deck, making a series of loud sounds.

What the hell was he doing?!

Even Ji Yuan was stunned by Du Changsheng’s shameless actions and immediately issued an Edict to Du Changsheng.

“Freeze!”

In an instant, Du Changsheng’s face, which was full of surprise and wrinkled with joy, froze in place. After all, Du Changsheng also possessed spiritual energy and supernatural power, but it was only at this moment that Ji Yuan’s eyes truly recognized who this person was.

“You are… the Heavenly Master Du, appointed by Emperor Yuan De?”

Hu Yun jumped over from the bow of the ship, looking at Du Changsheng with amazement. Du Changsheng’s face was all scrunched up with wrinkles but he didn’t move at all. Hu Yun stretched out his paw and pressed Du Changsheng’s face with his paw pad. He found that it did not resemble human flesh at all. Instead, it was like stacked layers of old cowhide, hard and unyielding.

“Mr. Ji, how did you do that? Why can’t he move?”

Bai Qi, standing beside him, was equally astonished; like Hu Yun, this was the first time he had ever seen a technique that could completely immobilize someone.

I considered translating this as “telekinesis,” but that seems really out of place

Chapter 433: The Heavenly Master Still Wants To Climb Higher

Even when completely frozen in place, one would not be completely devoid of consciousness; one’s spiritual senses would remain intact. Du Changsheng could see, hear, and even feel Hu Yun’s claws pressing on his face. However, to Hu Yun, this person was like a statue.

Ji Yuan stood up, walked around the table, and approached the frozen Du Changsheng and the terrified Wang Xiao. He then spoke to Du Changsheng.

“Don’t casually call me your master again, or I’ll freeze you and throw you into the Chunmu River to sober you up!”

Ji Yuan had met many people in his life, including many cultivators, whether human or ghost, god or demon, but he had never seen anyone as adept at climbing up the ladder as Du Changsheng. No, it was more accurate to say that he was able to come up with a ladder himself and climb up it.1

Ji Yuan did indeed give him the “Minor Qi Guiding Art” technique, but aside from the fact that this technique was a basic method in the cultivation world, it was also an equivalent exchange for Ji Yuan, as he learned the technique for the incomplete Warrior Talisman. In terms of sects that had been established due to the “Minor Qi Guiding Art,” it would almost be more accurate to say that Du Changsheng was an apprentice of Yuhuai Mountain.

As Ji Yuan spoke, he glanced at Wang Xiao beside him, sighed, and added a sentence.

“I am not your grandmaster.”

Then Ji Yuan looked at Du Changsheng again.

“I’ll withdraw the edict now. Do you understand what I said earlier?”

Seeing that Du Changsheng could neither nod nor respond, Ji Yuan figured that someone who wasn’t stupid should be able to understand. With that thought in mind, Ji Yuan moved, and the immobilization technique on Du Changsheng dissipated.

“Bang!”

Du Changsheng stumbled, and due to the momentum from his previous attempt to kowtow, his head slammed onto the deck again.

“Ouch… sir, this really wasn’t my intention, I didn’t mean to kowtow like that…”

Du Changsheng offered one explanation and then dared not say more, afraid of saying something wrong. He subconsciously rubbed his forehead. The sound of his head hitting the planks of the small wooden boat on the water was loud, but it wasn’t painful at all. Plus he was someone who had tempered his body with spiritual energy for many years, so his forehead did not even turn red.

Bai Qi, standing to the side, looked at Du Changsheng with amusement.

“So you really do know Mr. Ji, and you must know some of his abilities. Why do you dare to pretend to be Mr. Ji’s apprentice, and even dare to acknowledge him to his face?”

Bai Qi found this simply unbelievable. A mediocre cultivator like Du Changsheng was no different from an ordinary person in front of him, let alone Mr. Ji.

Du Changsheng glanced at Ji Yuan cautiously and explained.

“Back then, I was struggling with no way to cultivate, and my end of days was drawing ever closer. It was Mr. Ji who bestowed upon me a righteous method, giving me hope… Therefore, I have always regarded Mr. Ji as my master in my heart…”

Ji Yuan smiled and shook his head, then said to Bai Qi.

“That is true. However, back then, I only thought that Master Du’s talismanic arts were somewhat interesting, so I asked to take a look in exchange for the ‘Minor Qi Guiding Art’ method. I never thought of taking him as a disciple.”

Bai Qi burst into laughter.

“This Master Du has good judgement. He recognized a high branch to climb on the social ladder and went straight for someone like Mr. Ji.”

Du Changsheng dared not speak carelessly. Meanwhile, Wang Xiao on the side was even more nervous. He used to think that his master Du Changsheng was already a very remarkable master, and his understanding of immortals was basically limited to those in temples, which he considered unattainable.

Du Changsheng still had some principles in this regard. He didn’t lie to his disciples, and he would never claim to be a god. He also knew that he was not skilled enough to be considered a true immortal, and perhaps even not at the level that could be called “immortal” in any respect.

But now, Wang Xiao had met Bai Qi and Ji Yuan. The former was the God of Chunmu River, who ruled over such a vast river and was a deserving deity. The latter, seeing how this River God treated him with respect and courtesy, must be a True Immortal.

Wang Xiao dared not look too closely at the powerful figures beside him, but he frequently glanced at a red fox right next to Du Changsheng. This fox could talk, so it must be a monster, but it looked harmless.

At this moment, Hu Yun was looking up at Du Changsheng. Seeing that he could squish his head again and his body temperature had returned to normal, he became even more curious about Ji Yuan’s immobilization technique. He jumped to the side of the boat to talk to his friend in the water.

“Qingqing, did you see that just now? Mr. Ji shouted ‘Freeze,’ and that guy stopped moving. He was stiff and had no pulse. Now he’s alive again.”

Ji Yuan almost choked at Hu Yun’s words. What did he mean by “now he’s alive again”? He hadn’t killed Du Changsheng just now.

“Blub, blub, pop…”

The big black carp kept blowing bubbles. Even the sound of the bubbles popping was much louder, as if to show its agreement with Hu Yun.

It was only then that Du Changsheng and Wang Xiao noticed the movement in the water. Glancing to the side, they saw a large old turtle, about half the size of a painted boat, floating on the surface of the water with its black back visible through the ripples. A similarly enormous black carp was swimming near the side of the boat.

Forget Wang Xiao, even Du Changsheng had never seen a real monster before. Both of them were a mix of fearful and excited, but they could only hold it in.

Ji Yuan found it amusing and softened his tone.

“Alright, everyone sit down, let’s go over the ins and outs of this matter.”

A moment later, the small table on the ship was moved to a more spacious spot next to the cabin door. Ji Yuan and Bai Qi sat down one after the other, while Du Changsheng and Wang Xiao sat down on either side, looking somewhat reserved.

“It’s good that Master Du understands the implications. This sect isn’t one you can just casually claim. There’s no need to be overly nervous right now. We are all righteous cultivators and won’t do anything to you. Tell me about your situation.”

“Yes, yes, yes!”

Du Changsheng nodded repeatedly, his posture like that of a student in a private school. He knew that although he was already in his seventies or eighties, the two people in front of him were the real centenarians.2 Although they both looked middle-aged or younger, and the traces of time were invisible unless he looked closely, they must have lived for at least several hundred years, or even more.

“Since the late emperor passed away, the new emperor, though also curious about supernatural matters, lost interest after visiting us few Heavenly Masters remaining in the capital. Of course, while we could still enjoy wealth and honor in the capital, I, Du, having traveled the righteous path, knew that I should prioritize righteous cultivation. Even though I was somewhat reluctant to give up wealth and honor, I left the capital…”

After organizing his thoughts, Du Changsheng briefly recounted his story, though he unconsciously chose to say things that were advantageous for himself. For example, he said that he left the capital because he wanted to cultivate properly, but he had also been ridiculed by his fellow Heavenly Masters and couldn’t stay any longer.

“I was used to spending money lavishly in the capital, so I haven’t saved up much. Chunhui Prefecture is the most famous prefecture along the Chunmu River, and property here alone is quite expensive, so I’m a bit strapped for cash…”

At this point, Du Changsheng quickly offered an explanation.

“I don’t spend money extravagantly because I want to have fun. Sir and the Lord River God may not know the hardships of us independent cultivators. As the saying goes, ‘the poor study literature, the rich study martial arts,’ and we cultivators also have the saying: ‘wealth, methods, companions, and land.’ Reclusive masters live in blessed sacred lands and sacred caverns and are naturally carefree, but we have to worry about making a living…”

Ji Yuan nodded. He was aware of this. There were many “experts” in various places. Some were just going through the motions, but many of them had some real skills. Ji Yuan had encountered them a few times, such as the one on the beach in the country of Zuyue, and Du Changsheng and his deceased master. All of the above could be considered to be independent cultivators.

“Although I am relatively low-key, after handling some matters, I gained some fame in Chunhui Prefecture. Later, Li Jinlai came to me and told me about the Wei family’s divine fish statue, hoping to get his aid as well…”

Du Changsheng glanced at the large black carp by the side of the boat before continuing.

“I had heard about the giant fish in Chunmu River that saved people before. I knew that if this was true, it must be a virtuous aquatic creature. Of course, it wouldn’t harbor any intent to harm anyone. So I compromised and devised a method. I didn’t expect Li Jinlai to actually succeed. Later, the River God suddenly visited…”

Ji Yuan didn’t say anything after he had finished listening. He wasn’t thinking about the second half of Du Changsheng’s words about the big black carp, but rather about the first half of Du Changsheng’s story, and about the handful of Heavenly Masters in the capital.

Bai Qi simply drank his tea. He had no interest in such trivial matters. Meanwhile, Du Changsheng and Wang Xiao waited quietly.

After a long silence, Ji Yuan finally spoke.

“Since Master Du has accepted the title from the imperial family of Dazhen, you have already put your lot in with the country. The situation in Dazhen has been improving in recent years. When you realize that you and Dazhen now share mutual glory and prosperity, and your fates are linked, you will also be able to make progress in your cultivation.”

Du Changsheng forced a smile.

“That also means it’s a matter of mutual destruction…”

Previously, he had little hope for advancement, so when he had the chance to be granted the title of Heavenly Master, of course he wanted to compete for it. But now, the title of Celestial Master was meaningless, especially since Emperor Yuande had passed away.

“With its current momentum, if no extraordinary external influences interfere, Dazhen will experience at least another hundred years of national fortune. If you can embark on the path of immortal cultivation before your lifespan runs out, then you will naturally need to concentrate on cultivation and stabilize your practice. Once your cultivation is stable, you might as well wholeheartedly serve as the Heavenly Master of Dazhen.”

Du Changsheng frowned and pondered for a moment. His heart skipped a beat, and he looked up at Ji Yuan.

“Sir, do you mean that the dynasty of Dazhen’s national fortune can actually begin to rise again after two hundred years?”

Ji Yuan smiled but didn’t speak, while Bai Qi put down his teacup and said leisurely.

“Have you ever heard of the divination performed by Tianji Pavilion that predicted the coming of great prosperity for the land of Dazhen?”

Du Changsheng honestly shook his head.

“I have never heard of it; I don’t even know what Tianji Pavilion is.”

“Haha, this matter is no longer a secret among those who walk the divine path in Dazhen. As for Tianji Pavilion, it is a celestial abode renowned for its ability to peer into the secrets of heaven, located in an outdoor sacred cavern.”

Du Changsheng could only nod; it sounded incredibly impressive.

However, after this explanation, Du Changsheng had nothing more to say. He couldn’t get a word in edgewise with two experts around. Fortunately, Ji Yuan was considerate enough to allow some questions about cultivation and gave him a few pointers before sending him back.

This time, River God Bai Qi didn’t need to personally escort Du Changsheng back. Instead, another painted boat appeared nearby at some unknown time, carrying Du Changsheng and his disciple back. The boatman was naturally a transformed aquatic creature.

After Du Changsheng left, Bai Qi finally couldn’t help but blurt something out.

“That old fox!”

“Haha, only a cunning old fox like him can get by in the court and in this country. Who else would be willing to gamble their own cultivation on the fate of an entire dynasty?”

Upon seeing Du Changsheng, Ji Yuan recalled the fleet of ships he had encountered at sea before. That fleet was being guided by the national advisor of their respective country to search for Xianxia Island.

Chapter 434: A Sky Full of Glowing Energy

“Mr. Ji, what technique did you use to freeze that old man just now? Would you mind letting me know?”

Bai Qi finally asked the question. He only wanted to understand, not to pry into the true nature of his method, so shouldn’t be a big problem.

Hearing this, Hu Yun also pricked up his ears, as did the Old Turtle and the big black fish in the water.

Ji Yuan reached out and touched his teacup, drawing out a thin stream of water. At the same time, he picked up a chopstick from the table and stood it upright. The stream of water drew a circle in the air, binding the chopstick.

Ji Yuan let his hand drop, and the chopsticks, bound as they were by the stream of water, stood upright in mid-air.

“This is using the flow of water to shape and bind objects. There is also a method to bury objects in the earth. These are the basic binding principles. They mostly rely on tangible objects, but there is a kind of supernatural technique that can use intangible power to make the spirits of all things, whether in heaven, earth, or man, obey its command.”

Bai Qi frowned and asked tentatively.

“Sir, are you referring to the Edict technique?”

“Yes and no.”

Ji Yuan tried to explain what he knew in a way that others could understand, but considering that Bai Qi was a water dragon, and Hu Yun, the old turtle, and the big black fish were also monsters, it was basically impossible for them to learn how to use Edicts in the way of the immortal path, so he simplified it further.

“To put it simply, although an Edict is composed of written text and Taoist sounds, it is essentially a kind of sound command technique. Even a written Edict still needs to be recited by the caster. However, a written edict can be given by someone with higher Taoist cultivation to another, which lowers the requirements for the practitioner.”

Bai Qi nodded. Although Hu Yun and the other monsters didn’t understand some of the details, they grasped the general meaning and direction of his words, so they weren’t too confused.

Ji Yuan continued.

“However, you have only ever heard of Edicts, you aren’t aware of how they originated. You are not unique in this respect, as many immortal cultivators who study Edicts are also only taught the orthodox method by their masters. Not even they know the origin of the Edict technique.”

Bai Qi replied solemnly.

“I’m sure you know of it, sir. I’d like to hear the details.”

“Yes, I, Hu Yun, would also like to hear the details!”

Ji Yuan smiled. This fox and Bai Qi seemed to get along quite well. Thinking back to the feeling he had when he obtained the “Public Records of Emperor Zhengde,” he carefully considered his words before speaking.

“In fact, everything in the world has its own principles and laws. Among them, the world itself has its own Tao sound, which complements the world’s laws. The Edict technique is actually a way to manipulate this kind of spiritual power, which is as unpredictable as the might of heaven, and also extremely difficult to control.”

Although Bai Qi himself was not skilled in the art of Edicts, he could sense just now that Ji Yuan was not using an Edict, or at least it didn’t look like one to him. Indeed, as Mr. Ji said, the art of Edicts was very mysterious, but it was also traceable, and there were at least ways to combat it.

If Du Changsheng were restrained by an Edict just now, it would have been like the chopsticks on the table that had been tied with the circular stream of water just now: the binding would be “visible,” and thus could be untied. But just now… there were no bindings.

“Sir, the method you used does not resemble an Edict!”

Bai Qi hesitated for a moment before finally speaking his mind.

“Indeed, this was not an ordinary edict. You couldn’t tell where this Edict originated, could you?”

Bai Qi nodded in agreement. The Old Turtle seemed to be deep in thought as well, while Hu Yun and the big black fish could only listen to the excitement and try to remember.

Showing off one’s knowledge could actually be quite rewarding, and even Ji Yuan was no exception. However, this was only up to a certain point; he stopped being mysterious and went straight to the point.

“Cultivators often say that there is a large universe outside the body and a small universe inside the body. All living things have flaws and imperfections in their inner world, but the human body is uniquely complete. In general, if there is a world within the body, what if I could ‘command’ this inner world to manifest?”

Bai Qi was immediately stunned. One’s own inner world could be commanded? Or rather, it could be commanded by someone else? This kind of supernatural power was terrifying…

“It’s not as powerful as you think. It’s still limited by the opponent’s cultivation level. The higher the cultivation level, the stronger the spiritual power, and thus the less affected they would be. If their cultivation level were higher than mine, then it would naturally have no effect.”1

Hearing this, Bai Qi forced a smile and offered a sincere compliment.

“This is truly a miraculous technique; I have never heard of it before.”

Ji Yuan was quite pleased by his words. Most of the skills he possessed were self-developed, which gave him a great sense of accomplishment. It was only natural for him to appreciate being praised.

“You wouldn’t have ever heard anything about it. This technique is a method that I devised myself, and I’m sure there’s no one else in the world who knows it.”

…

Several dozen li away from the small boat where Ji Yuan and the others were gathered, a small painted boat was speeding towards Chunhui Prefecture. The boatman’s rowing was even and powerful, providing strong forward momentum to the small boat.

Thanks to this, the boat was able to reach the docks quickly. As they approached the pier, they could see the shoreline bustling with people, and the various sounds grew increasingly lively.

Only then did Du Changsheng breathe a slight sigh of relief, and Wang Xiao also visibly relaxed. As the boat approached the shore, the Du Changsheng looked at the boatman rowing and asked with a smile.

“Sir, are you a member of the aquatic creatures of the river?”

The boatman, wearing a long, thick raincoat and a large bamboo hat that covered almost his entire body, looked up at Du Changsheng when he heard his words.

“Haha, that’s right. I am an aquatic monster under the command of Lord River God. At present, the power of the sun is too strong to easily transform into human form. My appearance is frightening, so I covered myself with a straw raincoat and bamboo hat to avoid startling you two.”

Du Changsheng respectfully cupped his hands to the boatman, and Wang Xiao beside him followed suit.

“Thank you for seeing us off, sir.”

This aquatic monster was probably not too far off from transforming into human form, or perhaps it had already transformed but found its form very ugly?

“No need to be so polite. I was just following orders. Please take care, masters.”

As he spoke, the small boat came to a smooth stop beside a set of bluestone steps at the dock. Du Changsheng and Wang Xiao bowed again before quickly stepping onto the steps and onto the solid bluestone dock.

“Phew…”

Du Changsheng breathed a sigh of relief. He turned back around and looked again to see that the small boat that had just brought the two of them back had slowly left the dock, turning its bow around and heading toward the distant river.

“Master, those two must have been deities, right?”

Du Changsheng nodded and said in a low voice.

“Indeed, one was a god, and the other is an immortal. Alas, what a pity. If Mr. Ji could allow me to gain even the slightest recognition, or even just let me use his name, I would benefit greatly in the future.”

However, despite his sigh, Du Changsheng was still in a good mood afterward.

“Let’s go back. Although Mr. Ji won’t take us in, he still pointed us in the right direction. It all depends on whether your master can break through. I want to live for several more decades… hmm, preferably several more centuries!”

With a flick of his sleeve, Du Changsheng led Wang Xiao away from the dock and headed towards Chunhui Prefecture. As they walked, Du Changsheng touched a cloth bag in his robes. Inside was a round gold plaque, about half the size of a palm, bestowed by Emperor Yuande of Dazhen, known as the Heavenly Master’s Golden Token.

‘Thank goodness we still have this!’

Du Changsheng thought to himself in relief. He had previously planned to have the heavy gold token melted down into a gold ingot by a blacksmith if he could break through.

As the two walked, the voices of the people around them suddenly became noisy. Although it had been lively before, the voices were now clearly filled with astonishment.

“Look at the sky!”

“Mother, Mother, look at the sky! Father, look at the sky!”

“What? Oh my god, what is this?”

“Ah… everyone, look at the sky!”

“Oh my, is this a miracle from the heavens?”

While everyone was looking up at the sky, a thief took advantage of the moment and crouched down, happily picking some people’s money bags. He thought to himself, “it’s not thundering or raining, how can looking up at the sky bring good fortune?”

Then he subconsciously looked up and was immediately stunned.

Du Changsheng and Wang Xiao, who were standing to the side, also looked up upon hearing the sound, and were equally astonished.

“Master, what is that?”

“I…I don’t know either…”

High in the sky, streaks of light, like rainbows and flowing clouds, flashed across the horizon, tracing graceful paths across the sky. There were quite a few of these trails, countless streaks of light passing through the sky.

Du Changsheng channeled all his spiritual power into his eyes, and for a moment the light in the sky became even more dazzling. Ordinary people could only see thin, colorful ribbons of light flashing by, but Du Changsheng could see a kaleidoscope of colors painting half the sky.

Somewhere on the Chunmu River, Ji Yuan and Bai Qi stood up from their small boat at the same time, looked up at the sky, and gazed at the dazzling light that filled the heavens high above.

“Sir, what is this?”

Ji Yuan’s expression was serious. The sky was filled with Dharma light, which even ordinary people could see. This was because the aura was too strong, with too many people wielding spiritual energy. The aura light came from the southeast; Ji Province was already in the southeast part of Dazhen, and beyond this point there were only two neighboring prefectures.

There were no powerful immortal sects in these regions, and these spiritual lights could not have come from Yuhuai Mountain. They could only have come from a more distant location, perhaps even beyond the sea.

“I don’t know what happened, nor do I know what kind of beings these people in the sky are. However, they are likely immortal cultivators.”

“Sir, what do you intend to do?”

Bai Qi asked urgently, knowing that Mr. Ji couldn’t possibly remain unmoved by the scene.

Ji Yuan glanced down at him.

“I’ll go after them and see. If I don’t return quickly, please help me send Hu Yun back to Ju’an Pavilion or Niu Kui Mountain.”

Before he could finish speaking, Ji Yuan had already leaped into the air. The small boat beneath him didn’t even sway. When Ji Yuan was more than ten feet above the deck, the Green Vine Sword flew down to his feet.

Then, with a flash of sword light, Ji Yuan disappeared from his original spot, leaving only a streak of light as he flew away into the sky.

This was Ji Yuan’s fastest movement technique, using the power of the Immortal Sword and simultaneously riding the wind to fly.

T/n: everyone else’s thoughts at this moment: so basically no one in the world is safe, Mr. True Immortal Who Can Command the World itself

Chapter 435: Xianxia Island Cultivators

Bai Qi hesitated for a moment, but ultimately did not chase after him. He looked down at Hu Yun, the Old Turtle, and the big black fish by the boat, said nothing, then continued to look up at the sky, frowning in thought.

In just a few breaths, the light in the sky disappeared, or at least the people in Chunhui Prefecture could no longer see it. Only Bai Qi could see the light on the horizon receding into the distance.

In the sky, Ji Yuan swifly traveled on his flying sword, but he had no intention of flying directly to the front to block the path and inquire. It would be like using a car to block someone who was clearly in a hurry. So Ji Yuan planned to follow along behind them and gradually approach.

The rosy light in the sky was further away than expected, but Ji Yuan’s flight speed was also quite fast, approaching the light an upward parabolic trajectory.

Once he reached a suitable distance, Ji Yuan could sense the immortal energy within the rosy light, confirming that this was indeed the work of a group of immortal cultivators.

Of the heavenly auras in the sky, the six at the front were the most dazzling. These six rays of light dominated the heavenly radiance, almost spreading their Taoist auras to cover the area behind them, allowing the glowing light behind them to keep up with their speed.

These six beams of light were actually six immortal cultivators riding on colorful clouds, a mix of both male and female, of varying ages.

Suddenly, one of the old men in the middle had a thought and looked down and to the side behind him. The next moment, the other five also turned their heads to look in the same direction.

Although they couldn’t see anything there for the time being, and though their senses were sometimes even obscured by thick clouds and fog, they could clearly sense a faint but sharp force approaching.

Swish…

A sword light pierced through the clouds. Although it was still far away, it was rapidly approaching, heading directly towards the front, where the six cultivators were located.

“Such a fast sword light! Are there any powerful immortal abodes, sects, or cultivators here?”

The beams of aura light continued unabated as the old man in the middle spoke calmly, questioning those around him. Hearing his question, a woman beside him thought for a moment and then answered.

“This place is a small country in the southern part of the Eastern Continent, but if we are discussing about immortal abodes and sects, there is indeed one which should be in this vicinity. I think it’s called… Yuhuai Mountain?”

“Yuhuai Mountain is not known for sword cultivators, is it?”

“Even so, it’s not surprising that there would be powerful sword cultivators in the sect.”

Another elderly man thought for a moment.

“As far as I know, Yuhuai Mountain is not a sect-type immortal abode. There is no sect leader or similar figure in charge. It tends more toward a path where each branch flourishes, so it is possible that there are powerful sword cultivators among them.”

“Please be patient, everyone. That person is here.”

As soon as he finished speaking, all six fell silent. However, the speed at which the flying radiant light approach did not decrease at all, and the other flying immortal cultivators also began to notice Ji Yuan’s sword light one after another.

Looking around at this moment, Ji Yuan saw dazzling colors everywhere, as radiant as the clouds at sunset, divided into various colors. Once he had caught up, he directly sheathed his immortal sword and approached on clouds to show his peaceful intent.

The surrounding glow did not hinder Ji Yuan; instead, everyone watched the white-robed figure walking on the clouds with curiosity. Many scrutinized him, but suddenly realized that they could only see the person physically, and could not sense his aura. They immediately understood that the newcomer’s cultivation level was extremely high.

This was the first time in Ji Yuan’s life that he had seen so many immortal cultivators in one place, especially those who were capable of using flight techniques to fly together.

These immortal cultivators’ expressions ranged from curious to serious, from indifferent to simply ignoring him. Many of them were not riding on clouds or wind, but rather on something that radiated light, like a colorful fog of aura, which explained its dazzling brilliance.

Ji Yuan approached the six people in front of him. They stood about four or five zhang apart, standing on a white cloud. All six people were watching at Ji Yuan. The old man in the middle started first before Ji Yuan could speak.

“May I ask who you are, fellow Taoist? We are cultivators from Xianxia Island and are on an urgent journey. We apologize if we have disturbed you.”

The island cultivators were shimmering with light, and the sky was ablaze with color. No wonder it was called Xianxia Island,1 Ji Yuan thought to himself. He cupped his hands slightly in greeting from where he stood on the cloud. Although the other party did not perform any gesture of formal greeting, Ji Yuan had almost developed a conditioned reflex to this kind of etiquette.

“Fellow Taoists of Xianixa Island, my surname is Ji. I do not belong to any particular immortal sect. I am merely a local who was drinking tea down below. Seeing the colorful clouds filling the sky, I came to see what was happening. If it is convenient, would you all be willing to tell me?”

Only Ji Yuan would possibly use the term “local,” but the cultivators from Xianxia Island didn’t pay attention to such details. Seeing Ji Yuan’s unfathomable cultivation and polite demeanor, a woman beside him returned the greeting first, and then spoke.

“One branch of our Xianxia Island encountered a dragon vein overflowing with malevolent energy. When they tried to seal it off, it attracted many evil demons. They are still fighting against them, we are rushing to their aid.”

“How far are they?”

Ji Yuan asked almost instinctively. The cultivators from Xianxia Island glanced at Ji Yuan several times. Although his power and divine light were not obvious, the peaceful aura around him still indicated that he was a legitimate cultivator.

“It’s still about 100,000 li away, quite a journey.”

Ji Yuan hesitated for a moment, glanced down in the direction of Chunhui Prefecture and Chunmu River, and then made a decision in his heart. Whether from his own sense of mission or curiosity, such opportunities were rare, so he replied to the cultivators in front of him.

“Since you, fellow cultivators, were attacked by demons while sealing the evil energy of the earth’s veins, if you don’t mind, would you allow me to accompany you and do my part?”

The gale they had induced through their hurried journey continued to tear at the surrounding rosy clouds. The six cultivators from Xianxia Island exchanged glances, while some frequently scrutinized Ji Yuan and fixed their gaze on the spiritually powerful immortal sword behind him.

This was clearly a sword cultivator, or rather, a fellow immortal cultivator with considerable skills in sword cultivation and remarkable abilities. After all, with an immortal sword to rely on, such immortal cultivators possessed extremely strong lethal techniques.

Although the cultivators of Xianxia Island believed they were strong enough for this trip, since this fellow Taoist had shown goodwill, it could be considered a fortunate turn of events.

The old man in the middle returned the greeting.

“Since you have such good intentions, fellow Taoist, please come with us!”

The other five cultivators from Xianxia Island who were leading the group also bowed to express their gratitude. One of them, a man with a scholarly appearance standing in the middle, felt that his attire was most similar to Ji Yuan’s, and spoke up at that moment.

“In that case, fellow Taoist, please come closer to my radiant light. Um, I hope you will not resist the Radiant Cloud Array’s inspection.”

Ji Yuan realized that this group of immortal cultivators had set up an array. They were indeed extraordinary to be able to set up an array while flying and traveling.

“Haha, I have no intention of resisting.”

Ji Yuan chuckled, then rode his cloud toward the six people. The cloud was swept into the colorful light, and apart from a gentle breeze, he felt nothing unusual. Ji Yuan quickly flew to the side of the scholar who had just spoken.

Seeing that Ji Yuan entered the Radiant Cloud Array so casually and without any other reaction, the others lowered their guard slightly, but also secretly re-evaluated Ji Yuan.

Upon entering the so-called Radiant Cloud Array, Ji Yuan discovered that riding the clouds was much less strenuous; he barely needed to use any spiritual power to maintain a steady and fast pace as he flew.

But upon further thought, it made sense. These people from Xianxia Island were rushing to provide support. If they expended too much spiritual energy along the way, they would arrive exhausted.

In any case, since the Radiant Cloud Array did not react to Ji Yuan, everyone stopped thinking about it for the time being. The immortal cultivators from Xianxia Island behind them were slightly curious about the cultivator who had just arrived, but apart from exchanging whispers among themselves, they did not react in any other way. The group resumed their quiet flight.

The scholarly man beside Ji Yuan bowed slightly to him.

“My name is Chang Yi from Xianxia Island. Since your attire is similar to mine, I won’t address you as fellow Daoist, but instead I’ll call you Mr. Ji.”

Ji Yuan returned the bow.

“My name is Ji Yuan. I apologize for the intrusion! To be honest, Mr. Ji is what everyone I know calls me.”

Chang Yi smiled and nodded, his gaze sweeping over the Green Vine Sword behind Ji Yuan, and asked.

“Is Mr. Ji carrying a spiritually-enlightened Immortal Sword?”

The Green Vine Sword shifted slightly behind Ji Yuan, who nodded in response.

“It is indeed an immortal sword.”

The hilt of this immortal sword was actually entwined with vines, with tender buds at both ends of the cross-guard. A few vines also grew on the blade, and with its concealed sharpness, it looked lush and vibrant, full of vitality.

But this was, after all, an immortal sword. The more it appeared to be devoid of sword aura, the more extraordinary its power once unsheathed.

“I have long heard of the great name of Xianxia Island, but have never met anyone from there. Today, I had the good fortune to have encountered you.”

Ji Yuan also took the opportunity to inquire about Xianxia Island.

The two chatted with each other, and occasionally other cultivators nearby would chime in with a few words, which helped them get to know each other better.

For an immortal like Ji Yuan, who was clearly quite accomplished in cultivation, to live in a small mortal county without belonging to any sect or faction seemed a bit absurd. If someone else were to have informed them of this, the cultivators of Xianxia Island might not have believed it, but Ji Yuan’s aura was clear and bright, giving him a convincing presence.

They were even more surprised when Chang Yi suddenly discovered that Ji Yuan was actually blind, a fact confirmed by Ji Yuan himself. This was quite unbelievable for immortal cultivators.

After all, limb regeneration was not impossible through various supernatural means, so it should also be possible to restore his vision. However, Ji Yuan’s eyes were still blind, which meant there must be due to some kind of uncontrollable factor.

Ji Yuan also learned that these six were the six elders of Xianxia Island, leading a force of three hundred cultivators — and astonishingly large number, especially considering that they were from Xianxia Island — almost all of whom possessed considerable cultivation.

Perhaps due to the influence of Ji Yuan’s arrival, the cultivators of Xianxia Island deliberately used spiritual techniques to obscure the radiant glow of their clouds in the sky. Although to Ji Yuan, who was in the midst of the rosy light, still appeared magnificent, it was practically invisible from the ground.

After flying day and night without rest, the landscape below changed repeatedly, before finally, after about ten days, they arrived at a northeastern part of the Eastern Cloud Continent. A towering mountain appeared in front of them. Although it should have been daytime, the other side of the mountain was completely dark, without stars or moon.

In the depths of the mountain, a faint light flickered in the darkness.

“We’ve arrived.”

The leading elder looked solemnly at Ji Yuan, who had already moved to his side.

“Mr. Ji, allow us to borrow the power of your immortal sword to break through the fog!”

“As is my duty.”

Ji Yuan replied, his right hand already gripping the hilt of the Green Vine Sword. At this moment, the Edict inscription appeared on the scabbard, and the character “hidden” immediately disappeared.

The next moment.

“Shiiing—”

As soon as the sword rang out, the blazing white sword light, carrying boundless, sharp sword energy, swept across the land in a vertical, crescent-shaped slash.

Wherever it passed, the billowing black fog melted away like snow, splitting into two halves.

“Everyone, follow us to slay the demons and vanquish the monsters!”

The next moment, a burst of light appeared, flying over the mountains.

One possible translation is “Immortal Cloud Island” –> techniques look like sparkly clouds

Chapter 436: In the Depths of the Clouds, Unknown to Xianxia Island

A stray sword strike could accidentally injure the group of cultivators from Xianxia Island, so Ji Yuan’s sword light didn’t sweep along the mountain ridge, but instead rose upwards. Its main purpose was to clear away the fog and intimidate the demons, while also signaling to the trapped cultivators inside that reinforcements had arrived.

Within a hundred miles of that mountain range, demons and monsters roamed freely, fighting against the immortal cultivators. A massive fissure in the mountain range spewed out poisonous energy from the corrupted earth vein. The black mist that filled the sky was not only filled with evil demonic energy the demons, but was also mixed with this poisonous earth vein energy.

On either side of this unfathomably deep fissure, a massive Plum Blossom Array was radiating light. There were twelve points of light, with three or four immortal cultivators sitting cross-legged at each point, supporting the array with their spiritual power.

This grand formation not only protected those within it but also suppressed the evil energy emanating from the earth’s veins.

Around them, aside from scattered rocks and fallen trees, were numerous pits and hollows, some marked from burning flames, some marked with solidified ice crystals. Scattered among them was a considerable number of demon corpses, some large and some small, some burned, some beheaded, all kinds of deaths. It was a gruesome sight.

The surrounding area was filled with howling winds and eerie cries, a cacophony of wailing and raucous noises that were utterly disturbing.

“Boom…”

A small mountain collapsed, and a huge, hairy claw shattered the mountain peak, sweeping the boulders on the summit towards the Plum Blossom Array.

“Whoosh… Boom…”

The boulder shattered, but the Plum Blossom Array remained as solid as a rock, unmoved. However, at the very center, several cultivators frowned, and their auras became slightly unstable.

“Master, why aren’t they attacking us anymore? Are they afraid of us?”

At the center of the Plum Blossom Array, at one of its key points, a young cultivator asked his master this question. The master looked around, then back at the younger cultivator, and shook his head.

“No, the demons we killed in the previous battle were not particularly powerful. They were meant to wear down our spiritual power. The evil energy in this place is very toxic. Even demons who want to refine evil energy to forcibly improve their cultivation cannot fully withstand it. Our great formation here helps suppress the evil energy and bring it to a suitable level.”

“What!?”

The young cultivator almost stood up from his seated meditation posture, unable to accept the reality.

“Hehehehehe… Hahahaha… I didn’t expect this immortal to be able to see through it, hahahahahaha…”

“Hahahahaha…”

“Hehehehehehe…”

“Oh, hahahahaha…”

Suddenly, a massive burst of laughter erupted from the dark, shadowy distance, so loud and jarring that it even unsettled some of the weaker cultivators.

“Hahahaha… While you were hiding here like cowards, your mountain sect gates were completely destroyed, and all the cultivators left inside are dead!”

“Yes, yes, although their cultivation was weak, their immortal spirits were very pure, truly nourishing!”

“Yes, yes, especially those young boys and girls, their skin was so tender and delicate, they were such a nourishing tonic!”

“Hahahaha…”

The demons’ laughter never stopped, flooding the ears of the cultivators. Many of them, being discerning, quickly shut themselves off to avoid being affected by the mind-control technique.

“These wretched creatures!”

One cultivator could do nothing but mutter a curse, unable to leave the formation. They all knew how many demons were outside, and because of the array’s suppression, the earth vein’s poison was most concentrated near the ground.

Whether it was fighting against demons and monsters or being exposed to poisonous energy for extended periods of time, both were extremely dangerous and tantamount to courting death.

Even if the mountain gate was truly destroyed, they could not act rashly at this moment.

“Hmph, laugh, be arrogant…”

Within the Plum Blossom Array, a cultivator gritted his teeth and sneered. There were numerous demons in this group, and many more terrifying ones still lurked, hidden in the shadows.

Even if a naturally ruptured earth vein were to attract demons, it wouldn’t be this many, let alone so many terrifyingly powerful beings.

Nevertheless, the cultivators did not give up hope. The demons did not know that while they appeared to be just an obscure little sect in the region, they were actually a branch of Xianxia Island.

Even though they were completely isolated in these mountains, and even though their branch sect may have been destroyed, they could still notify Xianxia Island of their peril by shattering their life-bound talismans. Even if they died, the main sect would surely deliver a devastating blow to the demons.

For now, they would hold out for as long as they could!

On a high peak in the mountains stood a figure shrouded in black mist. He resembled a handsome official, with fine skin, clothing, and features, but he possessed an eerie and sinister aura. Standing beside him were many similar “people,” and upon closer inspection of the surrounding peaks, there were also quite a few individuals with demonic bodies trying to take human form.

“These so-called ‘immortals’ seem quite calm?”

“Haha, immortal cultivators cultivate both body and mind. Even if they are just from a small sect, how could we possibly break through their mindset with just a few words? Not to mention that they will definitely reassure themselves that their sect is safe; even if it were truly destroyed, they would still remain calm for a while.”

“Hahaha, this is good too. It helps us stabilize the earth veins, and we can kill them when the time is right.”

Listening to the demons and monsters around him laughing and joking, the extremely handsome demon in the middle smiled faintly and spoke in a cold voice.

“They are definitely waiting for an opportunity.”

“What opportunity?”

Someone asked. The handsome demon sneered.

“Naturally, an opportunity to counterattack. Otherwise, they would prefer to fight their way out and exhaust their spiritual energy than struggle down there. Immortal cultivators may be detached from the world, but they still have some fighting spirit.”

“Opportunity? Hah, like they still have a chance?”

A demonic creature in human form nearby sneered and mocked.

“There are no immortal sects for thousands of li. The mountain god of this mountain also tried to gather the earth’s veins, but we have already torn him to pieces. There are hardly any righteous sects around. Where is their chance?”

A glint of malice flashed in the handsome man’s eyes as he scanned his surroundings.

“Hahaha, we’ll have to wait and see then. I’m really looking forward to it.”

Just then, at this very moment…

Swish…

A dazzling sword light shone from afar, and in an instant, sword energy flashed across the land like a crescent moon. The surrounding darkness seemed to be swept away like a curtain being pulled back as the energy passed.

“Bang…”

“Bang…”

“Bang…”

“Bang…”

…

The sword light swept across the mountain peaks, shattering them and scattering rocks in all directions.

“Aaah…”

“What is this…?”

“Run!”

“Go…”

Swish…

Although the sword light swept vertically, it was not constrained to a small range. Coupled with its extremely high speed, any demons standing on the mountain peaks along the way were doomed, unless their cultivation was sufficient to avoid it in advance.

The demons on one of the last mountain peaks were almost directly confronted by the sword energy of the immortal sword head-on. The unparalleled aura swept over them, giving the demons a chilling feeling just at the sight of it, making many of them stiffen, unable to move.

With a whoosh, the sword light grazed the mountain peak and swept into the distant sky. Some demons trembled and squatted on the mountain, watching with lingering fear as the receding sword light pierced the darkness.

But this was not the end. In the very next moment, endless radiance appeared on the mountain range, and streaks of colorful clouds flew into the sky, dispelling the previous rolling darkness. The entire mountain range was illuminated with a magnificent and colorful light, lighting up the peaks and the land below.

“Cultivators of Xianxia Island, heed my command: slay any demons and monsters here without mercy, exterminate their forms and souls!”

A series of colorful clouds unfolded above the mountain range, each containing one to three cultivators from Xianxia Island. Some swept their whisks, some summoned magical artifacts, and some used their supernatural powers. In an instant, divine light burst forth, bringing annihilation to the demons and monsters who were still in shock from the earlier sword energy.

“Rumble…”

The sky turned dark again, but this time it was filled with thunderclouds.

“Crack… Boom…”

“Crack…boom…boom…”

Bolts of lightning struck down, sweeping across the mountain peaks.

The demons, slow to realize what was happening, finally reacted. Some turned tail and ran immediately, while others clashed with the cultivators from Xianxia Island.

Below, at the Plum Blossom Array in the fissure of the earth’s veins, the surviving cultivators looked at the sky filled with lightning and colorful clouds with gazes filled with renewed confidence.

“Our Xianxia Island has come to our aid!”

On many mountaintops, some of the more knowledgeable demons, including the handsome young man, looked at the rosy light and lightning spreading like a tide with slight astonishment.

“They’ve actually come from Xianxia Island in the Eastern Sea?”

Xianxia Island was quite famous and had gained a reputation, so much so that many demons had heard of it. Some demons had already given up, but others, who were on t he verge of a breakthrough after absorbing the poisonous energy, had been stimulated to the point of extreme arrogance.

“So what if it’s Xianxia Island? There are countless demons and monsters here, including many powerful demons. Even if cultivators from Xianxia Island have arrived, they’ll have to stay here! Roar…”

With a furious roar, the demon’s body grew taller and taller, and its demonic aura revealed its true form: a giant bear the size of a small mountain.

“Roar…”

The giant claw, shrouded in black light, slammed down, squarely striking two of the beams of light.

“Bang…”

“Boom…”

The two loud explosions were almost simultaneous. It was the sound of the two immortal cultivators being sent flying and crashing into the mountain peaks.

This successfully attracted the attention of many immortal cultivators, and in an instant, several bolts of heavenly lightning struck the giant bear.

“Crack, crack, crack…”

“Boom…”

“Bang…”

“Bang…”

Two earthen walls suddenly rose from the ground on either side of the giant bear, trapping him between them. Bolts of lightning struck down, paralyzing his body, followed by beams of light accompanied by fierce gales that swept across, skinning, ripping, and pulling out bones…

Only three of the immortal cultivators had made a move: Ji Yuan, the leading elder, the scholar, and a woman. They all landed on a high mountain peak and surveyed the area below. Their gaze swept over the surrounding mountain peaks. The obstructing darkness had thinned considerably, but they were still hidden in the black mist.

“A demon is a demon, after all. Instead of seeking arduous cultivation, they use such malevolent energy to help them break through. Sooner or later, they will face their own calamity.”

“Senior brother, there are probably many powerful demons lurking in the shadows around us.”

Hearing the woman’s words, the old man nodded.

“Yes, we must eradicate evil completely. Let’s get started. Mr. Ji, please stay here for now and assist us with your sword.”

“Alright!”

Ji Yuan agreed readily, showing no dissatisfaction at not being included in the attack. He watched as the three rays of light around him departed, heading straight for the edge of the mountain.

In Ji Yuan’s eyes, the cultivators of Xianxia Island were extremely organized at this moment. The number of cultivators in the center, the edges, and various locations remained almost even. The six elders also spread out in six directions, and the radiant array remained perfectly orderly. The colorful clouds also grew brighter and brighter.

To be honest, Ji Yuan felt that he didn’t need to take action at all, or rather, he couldn’t lend a hand. These immortal cultivators moved extremely fast and were very methodical within the formation.

Ji Yuan stood atop a solitary peak, sword in his left hand and both hands behind his back, witnessing the interplay of radiant clouds and lightning, and the rise and fall of demonic and evil energy.

Even if demonic blood and evil energy poured back onto this mountain peak, they would gently slide away around Ji Yuan.

On a distant mountain peak, several demons, including a handsome young man, remained still, coldly watching the immortal cultivators fight against other demons.

“My lord, shall we fight or retreat?”

The handsome young man’s gaze pierced through the thick fog and the numerous spiritual lights and demonic auras, looking at Ji Yuan on the distant solitary peak. All the filth and evil energy seemed to swirl around that peak.

“Although the cultivators from Xianxia Island who have already made their move are powerful, they are not the main concern. The person is the one we need to be most careful of, the one who hasn’t moved yet. That first sword strike must have come from him.”

As the handsome young man spoke, Ji Yuan seemed to sense a strange gaze. He slightly turned his head to look in the opposite direction. His pale eyes slightly opened, revealing his supernatural eyes, which met the handsome young man’s gaze from afar, causing the latter to tremble slightly.

“Yun Shen doesn’t know about Xianxia Island… Let’s retreat and let them fight here. We have gathered enough of the evil energy of the earth veins here.”

“Understood!”

“Yes, sir!”

The next moment, the demonic energy of the mountain peak flowed like water, gradually merging into the mountainside and disappearing.

But even after these demons left, many powerful demons and monsters remained. Although some noticed the handsome young man’s departure, most of them merely responded with cold laughter.

Chapter 437: Clash of Good vs Evil

Rays of light spread outwards, suppressing the demonic energy, monstrous energy, and most importantly, poisonous aura. Even while engaged in battle and deploying spiritual techniques, the cultivators of Xianxia Island maintained their grand formation.

“The scale of this earth vein poison is too large. Even though the ruptured earth vein is the source of this evil aura, it’s still a murky mess. Fortunately, the branch sect cultivators have suppressed it with formations. After so many days, the earth vein should have been stabilized.”

As the leading elder of Xianxia Island muttered to himself as he used hand seals and divine powers to sweep away a demon that was flying over the mountain peak. He looked at the numerous fissures of varying sizes across the landscape, covering a distance of several li. The largest fissure had been blocked by the immortal cultivators of the branch sect using an array, while other places still occasionally emitted poisonous energy.

Those demons who disliked orthodox cultivation lacked the mindset and understanding to comprehend the Tao of Heaven. However, this kind of evil energy from heaven and earth was their best elixir and external aid. It could not only help them break through cultivation bottlenecks, but also help them learn various demonic techniques and supernatural powers.

It could even delay their own potential calamities to some extent, but this delay was likely to snowball. If their cultivation was insufficient at that time, a minor calamity would turn into a major calamity, and a major calamity will turn into a death sentence.

“Hmph, let’s kill all these bastards first!”

There were indeed far more demons than expected, but this time, it wasn’t just a matter of good versus evil, but also a matter of the reputation of Xianxia Island. Even at the cost of casualties, they had to eliminate these demons.

The old man, riding on the colorful clouds, had already flown over a hundred li in total distance back and forth across the center of the Xianxia Island cultivators’ formation. The other five elders did the same, forming a standard hexagonal shape with a diagonal range of over two hundred li.

With their superior cultivation and special protective techniques, they killed the demons along the way if they could, and didn’t linger too long on those they couldn’t kill. They used their speed to travel quickly and directly fought their way to their current positions.

As if through telepathy, the six elders of Xianxia Island pointed to the sky at the same moment. The colorful clouds beneath their feet drifted out and flew higher into the sky above, then shone brightly.

As the new grand formation activated, streaks of colorful light swirling around it, and a scorching heat gradually rose from within.

Many demons, whether still hiding among them or engaged in fierce battles with the immortal cultivators, became enraged, recklessly unleashing their demonic powers.

One demon unfurled wings formed of demonic energy, leading a horde of demons soaring through the sky, battling four or five cultivators from Xianxia Island. It even held aloft a beating heart in its hand. It was unclear which cultivator had been slain.

As his agitation grew and his emotions became increasingly volatile, he finally noticed that the distant glow of the sunset had already risen like pillars of fire, extending in all six directions.

“Not good! Everyone, we have to break out of here quickly! That old bastard from Xianxia Island has set up a double formation. We can’t stay here any longer. Once the Radiant Light and the Blazing Fire ignite, no one will be able to escape!”

“Roar… Demons are demons, sneaking around and cowering. What use is the formation if we just kill all these so-called immortals! Awooo…”

A huge gray wolf howled to the sky, and the dark clouds that had originally been roiling with suddenly lightning parted, revealing a phantom bright moon.

The moon wasn’t real, as it was still daytime outside this mountain range, but the power of the moonlight indeed manifested. In an instant, the moonlight swept across the large valley, and the restlessness and burning sensation in the minds of the demons and monsters subsided considerably.

Then the enormous wave of demons roared, and the wolf’s head transformed into a phantom form, instantly leaping dozens of li to the left and right, like a wolf-shaped ghostly mist, darting among the immortal cultivators and demons. The next moment, without distinguishing between friend and foe, it directly swallowed up the creatures in an entire area.

However, just before the wolf-headed ghostly mist materialized, several colorful beams of light flew out from inside it.

“Hahahaha… Is this Xianxia Island, hidden deep within the clouds? Is this Changjian Mountain,1 famed for its unmatched strength? They’re nothing special! Awooo~!”

Many demons were initially still observing, but now that the six elders of Xianxia Island had unleashed a second formation, clearly intending to lock the gate and trap all the demons inside, they could no longer sit still.

In an instant, a large number of dark clouds of demonic energy rose up throughout the entire area of ​​radiant light, with demonic energy soaring into the sky and demonic flames raging.

Some powerful demons and monsters even used their supernatural powers to try to reverse the thunderclouds in the sky. Although they could not completely strip away the power to control the thunder, they tore open gaps of varying sizes in the clouds, making that part of the thunder useful to the demons.

“Crack… Boom…”

“Crack…crack…”

“Boom…”

“Boom…”

At that moment, at least dozens of lightning bolts struck the mountain peak where Ji Yuan stood. Clearly, many demons shared the same opinion as the handsome young man from before, believing that Ji Yuan was definitely the key figure of this campaign from Xianxia Island.

Ji Yuan, with his sword still clasped behind his back in his left hand, waved his hand toward the sky with his right, effortlessly absorbing the bolts of lightning into his sleeve. These bolts were then absorbed into the still-recovering Edict Lightning Talisman, without leaking a single spark.

Although he couldn’t use the lightning talisman at the moment due to that last heavenly tribulation, he had no problems absorbing the lightning controlled by these demonic arts.

At this moment, Ji Yuan stood on the mountaintop and surveyed his surroundings. Although the sky was still filled with radiant light and colorful clouds, it was clear that the number of demons far exceeded the number of Xianxia Island cultivators. Moreover, due to the power of the earth vein poison, the demons’ arrogance was still rising.

The initial fear the demons had shown toward Xianxia Island seemed of have lessened, especially after several powerful demons and monsters that had been lying low made their move, and were able to fight off many immortal cultivators on their own.

“Hmph!”

With a cold snort, Ji Yuan kept his left arm still, but grabbed the hilt of the Green Vine Sword with his right arm behind his back. The very next moment, he drew his sword.

“Shiiiing…”

The clear, resonant sound of a sword echoed across the land, followed by a fleeting but brilliant flash of sword light.

“Awooo…ugh…”

The giant wolf that had been roaring atop the mountain fell silent, caught off guard by the sudden and fatal blow from the Immortal Sword, as it joined the fierce battle alongside the three cultivators from Xianxia Island.

A line of blood appeared on the giant wolf’s neck. Then its massive head slowly slid off, plunging into the abyss below. Immediately afterwards, the huge wolf carcass followed alongside it, crashing against the mountainside with a thunderous roar.

Ji Yuan knew he couldn’t act rashly, not only because his spiritual power was ultimately limited, but also because he knew that many demons probably regarded him as the person in charge of Xianxia Island’s efforts here, and were secretly wary of him.

But that didn’t mean he couldn’t take action. On the contrary, he not only did he have to take action, but every time he did, he had to do so with thunderous force, otherwise it wouldn’t be enough to intimidate the demonic forces. He was currently looking for “key targets” on the battlefield; once he discovered a powerful demon who happened to be entangled with the cultivators from Xianxia Island, he would be ready to act.

Then, in the next moment, Ji Yuan would gather all his spiritual power, channel it into the Immortal Sword, and draw it to unleash a devastating strike. With this charged power, the Immortal Sword’s sharpness was unmatched.

Ji Yuan attacked several times in succession, each strike landing with unparalleled force. The demons struck showed no signs of injury; instead, they were utterly annihilated, body and soul.

To some demons who were filled with apprehension, this scene made it seem as if Ji Yuan were still holding back, as if he were merely observing out of boredom, occasionally swinging his sword to take a life.

This immediately dampened the demons’ arrogance, particularly the powerful ones. After fighting for so long and calming down, they began to have second thoughts.

The lightning that had initially targeted Ji Yuan was merely a small test. Then, seeing how casually Ji Yuan absorbed the lightning, and seeing how he occasionally began to attack from then on, it seemed as if he was only doing so because he was being annoyed by the lightning.

From then on, no demons directed attacks towards Ji Yuan. This was an invisible, unspoken fear, as if everyone naively believed that this would prevent them from provoking Ji Yuan.

Meanwhile, in the outer perimeter, a little further away from Ji Yuan, many powerful demons roared and unleashed their demonic power. Aside from resisting the supernatural powers and spiritual treasures of the immortal cultivators, their main purpose was to break Xianxia Island’s formations.

The six elders of Xianxia Island began to use their supernatural powers from the outermost edge of the boundary inwards, slowly advancing and accumulating a radiant and auspicious aura of fire.

Across every battlefield, they wielded wind, fire, lightning, and earth… The sunlight illuminated the collapsing mountains…

For hundreds of li all around, battles raged between immortal cultivators and demons. Some leaped and darted across the mountainside, while others flew and chased each other through the sky. Radiance and sharpness entwined, and roars and furious shouts echoed everywhere.

A demon, seemingly possessing a pair of bat wings billowing with smoke, glided swiftly between the mountain ridges, engaging in combat with the cultivators of Xianxia Island while exchanging simple conversations with some demons.

“Those six cultivators left for the outer boundary array. As long as we disrupt and shatter their inner formation, and then concentrate our efforts to break through in one place, we can escape.

“That is, provided that the man in the middle doesn’t make a move. Rather, let’s deal with that person in the middle first!”

A female demon, her entire body radiating a blood-red mist of demonic energy, flew down to their side on a demonic wind. She looked at the place where she knew Ji Yuan to be standing in the distance with slight apprehension. This far away, they could only see the mountains and not Ji Yuan himself, but they all knew that the swordsman was standing on that mountain peak, motionless from beginning to end.

At this point, the female demon seemed to have just remembered something.

“Where is the Northern Demon? Why hasn’t he made a move at this crucial moment? Didn’t he say he’d lure in the immortal cultivators so we could take them down together? Now that Xianxia Island has arrived, where is he?”

The demon who had sprouted bat wings sneered.

“If he hasn’t made a move now, he’s either hiding and waiting for us both to be exhausted, or he’s already run away. You can only trust the Northern Demon’s words so far!”

“And what about you? How much of what you say can I believe?”

The demon sneered but did not answer. Instead, he continued to channel his demonic energy towards the cracks in the ground.

“It’s not only immortals who can set up formations!”

At the same moment, it seemed that several demonic figures had already circled around to the vicinity of the various earth veins. Then, in the very next moment…

“Bang!”

“Bang!”

“Bang!”

…

An enormous portion of the earth’s evil energy, which had been gradually calming down, suddenly exploded into the sky, like a volcano erupting and spewing black smoke.

“Boom…”

The Plum Blossom Array from the branch of Xianxia Island was instantly swallowed up by the exploding malevolent energy, and the formation was shattered.

Many cultivators from Xianxia Island were using their spiritual techniques to slay demons and resist their counterattacks, but when they heard the violent tremor in the earth, as if an earth dragon were turning over, they instinctively looked down.

“Not good!”

“Get out of the way!”

“Get out of the way…”

Whether through the use of immortal techniques or life-saving supernatural powers and methods, streaks of light vanished in an instant. A few immortal cultivators with low cultivation and slow reaction, as well as the vast majority of demons who were entangled with them, were all swallowed up by the detonated poisonous energy at this moment.

It tore through the radiant light of Xianxia Island’s inner formation and pierced through the thunderclouds, continuing its ascent into the sky.

“Rumble…”

A ripple spread across the entire sky, as if it were vibrating constantly. Amidst the surging, malevolent energy, several beams of light emerged, alongside demonic lights. However, it was clear that those who had survived the impact—immortals, demons, and monsters alike—were all significantly affected, so much so that they did not engage in combat even when flying side by side.

“Now’s the time! Let’s go!”

Some demons and powerful monsters who had been waiting for this moment fled upwards along the pillar of malevolent energy…
