Swish swish swish swish swish…

Dozens of arrows flew into the distance. The ghost soldiers didn’t need to aim deliberately, the arrows just shot at the plague demons down on their own.

About a few dozen breaths later, an even louder roar came from outside the village.

“Kill them!”

“Kill!”

“Charge!”

…

In Maotan Village, whether it was Liao Daqiu, the old village chief, or even the numerous ordinary villagers, they all heard the sounds of fierce battle in their sleep. The sounds echoed throughout the village, as if the entire village were sleeping on a battlefield.

Some were awakened by this nightmare, but even after waking up, although it wasn’t as clear as in the dream, they could still faintly hear the sounds of fighting…

In the northwestern sky, a cloud flew through the night with a conspicuous spiritual light, its halo illuminating the surrounding area in a dazzling display.

Ji Yuan and Chang Yi deliberately revealed their spiritual light to notify any demons they might encounter of their presence. It was a move intended to alert them, whether to drive them out or drive them away. in any case, they did not want demons to linger in the mortal world.

“Hmm?”

Ji Yuan’s supernatural eyes swept across the landscape and seemed to see a deathly aura rising, looming and oppressive, in the distance. It perfectly fit the description of a deathly still atmosphere. Chang Yi clearly saw something as well.

“Mr. Ji, it seems that the situation in the mortal world is indeed unusual.”

“Let’s go!”

The cloud beneath them flashed with light, their speed immediately increasing as they flew off into the distance.

T/n: today I learned that the main difference between a graveyard and a cemetery is that a graveyard is traditionally associated with a church, while a cemetery is an independent and usually secular burial site. Neat. (I have updated previous chapters to match.)

The name used is 济命堂 (jì mìng táng), which literally translates to “Life Saving Hall”

Chapter 444: The Situation Is Urgent, Slay the Plague Demon

Ji Yuan and Chang Yi flew at a considerable speed, but the deathly atmosphere stretched over a vast area, so they could only fly to the nearest location to investigate.

As they drew closer, they could see the outlines of some towns and villages in the distance, but the human vitality was weaker than normal.

The night wind howled in the air, and the colorful clouds streaked across the sky in the darkness. The two people in the clouds observed the earth with their supernatural eyes and naturally noticed the peculiar ghostly light.

Chang Yi had more experience than Ji Yuan in this respect, and could immediately assess the situation.

“This is bad, Mr. Ji! This is a ghost born from the evil energy of heaven and earth. In addition, there must have been many corpses here, and the underworld has failed to handle them properly. The ghosts here have become a plague demon!”

The patches of ghostly light on the distant land all represented malevolent ghosts. They could even see underworld officials and deities throwing out techniques to fight them, but they were vastly outnumbered.

A ghostly green mist hung over the town before them, giving off an ominous feeling. Ghosts roamed the town, occasionally stealing wisps of yang energy from the inhabitants and emitting sharp cries that sounded both joyful and pained.

The glow in the night sky was extremely conspicuous. Although it was invisible to ordinary people, ghosts and spirits were very sensitive to it. Many ghosts and spirits had already looked up at the sky, but they couldn’t see anything aside from a single a colorful cloud.

Ji Yuan and Chang Yi circled the town and gained a general understanding of the ghosts’ activities. They no longer hesitated.

“Mr. Ji, you can just stand by and watch; I, Chang, will take the lead!”

As he spoke, Chang Yi had already formed a hand seal and cast a technique. His arms moved swiftly, his wrists twisting and turning, and in an instant, many beams of light flew downwards, instantly igniting any plague demons in their path.

A team of underworld officials, led by the judge, was fighting against hordes of plague demons. However, the approaching cloud in the sky was unsettling, and the spirits were also uneasy and unsure of what was going on.

“Your Honor, what is that glow in the sky? Could it be that someone is coming to our aid?”

“It’s still unclear, but a glow does not necessarily indicate a righteous cultivator; demons and evil spirits can also emit it, so we must be careful!”

As the judge answered, he swept his judge’s brush across the air, striking down the plague demons one by one and shaking their souls. Then, an underworld official would slay them with a single strike.

These plague demons were numerous and extremely malevolent, with a heavy, fierce aura. Often, after killing one, the evil energy wouldn’t dissipate, and instead would spread to other plague demons, potentially even spawning exceptionally powerful ones. Dealing with them required extreme caution.

Although ordinary plague demons weren’t particularly difficult to kill, after a prolonged struggle, even the ghosts and gods of the underworld felt exhausted. Everyone knew that plague demons were troublesome to deal with and had no energy left to control the situation.

Worse, the fiery Yang energy of ordinary people living together had little effect on the plague demons. The poisonous energy carried by the evil spirits had little effect on ordinary people living together, but once this poisonous energy spread, it could easily cause a plague. Infected people’s Yang energy could be devoured by the plague demons before death, agitating the group.

Furthermore, the corpses of these dead were even more greatly afflicted with the disease after death, becoming a breeding ground for the spread of plague; even without plague demons, people could still contract the disease.

These patterns had been gradually discovered over time, but the underworld currently had no capacity to do much about them now. Aside from sending messages to doctors, officials, and nobility in their dreams to offer advice on how to treat the plague, their main focus was on eliminating the plague demons. If the plague demons weren’t eliminated, every other action would be in vain.

Just as the ghosts and spirits were discussing the glowing clouds in the sky, beams of light suddenly appeared, sweeping across the area around the ghosts and spirits. The ghosts and gods were unharmed, but all the plague demons were set on fire.

“Aaaah…”

“Ugh…”

The plague demons, whose roars had remained unchanged no matter how they were hacked at, were now struggling and thrashing about in agony, letting out uncontrollable screams. Those plague demons that weren’t burned to death immediately set more and more of their brethren ablaze as they moved toward them.

The ghosts and gods instinctively stopped attacking and moved away from the plague demons. Then they looked up at the sky and saw two immortals standing on the clouds. One of them was making hand seals and casting a technique. The dazzling but extremely dangerous rays of light were emanating from his hand.

Soon, most of the plague demons in the city were engulfed in the flames brought about by the rosy light. At that moment, Chang Yi changed his hand seal and raised his hand upwards.

Boom…

All the plague demons below burst into flames in an instant, and were burned to ashes in the span of just two breaths.

Besides observing the plague demons below, Ji Yuan also paid close attention to the entire process of Chang Yi’s technique. Chang Yi’s technique was a feast for the eyes, practically an art form. If it weren’t for the current situation, Ji Yuan probably would have been in a better mood and wanted to learn more.

“Thank you for your supernatural assistance, Immortal Masters. May I ask which immortals you two are? We are local underworld spirits and deities, and we have been battling these plague demons for several days. However, this place is only one of the areas ravaged by the plague demons, and there are many other places where they roam…”

The judge clasped his hands and explained the current situation to the two immortals on the clouds, hoping they could lend a helping hand. His expectations soon became reality.

Ji Yuan and Chang Yi returned the greeting simply, with the former saying:

“The name ‘Plague Demon’ is quite fitting for these creatures. They were likely brought about due to the evil energy from the earth veins. We will naturally try to resolve it. We also ask the underworld spirits to search again to see if there are any that have escaped.”

After finishing his explanation, Ji Yuan turned to Chang Yi.

“Mr. Chang, let’s split up and search for these demons, and try to get rid of them all. These demons, as the embodiment of plague, are far more harmful to this world than ordinary demons and monsters.”

Ji Yuan’s experience from his previous life told him that these kinds of ghosts were actually quite troublesome. While ordinary demons could be eliminated, these ghosts might have already spread plague, and could even be carried to more distant places, becoming human-borne instead of supernatural.

“We’ll do as you say, sir!”

Chang Yi responded, and in the next moment he and Ji Yuan transformed into two streaks of light and flew away in different directions, having no time to chat with the ghosts and gods below.

In this small region in the northwest of the country of Yuanzhao, the plague had clearly already spread. There were an enormous number of plague demons.

Ji Yuan was not as skilled in eliminating plague demons as Chang Yi, but he still had his own efficient methods. Wherever he went, he directly used the sword energy of his immortal sword to kill the plague demons. He didn’t care whether it was like using a cannon to kill a mosquito, he only knew that this method was absolutely effective.

The area ravaged by the plague demons was actually much larger than Liao Daqiu and the others had imagined. In the past few days, they had only heard about it in terms of “the surrounding counties” or “neighboring towns,” as if it were just a few counties. But Ji Yuan and Chang Yi flew across the region tonight, the area of which could be counted as a prefecture. Although they didn’t know the regional names of this country, Ji Yuan even wondered if he had been traversing the equivalent of half of Ji Province to slay demons.

Fortunately, Ji Yuan’s supernatural eyes were special. At this moment, relying on his pure spiritual power, he constantly observed heaven and earth in all directions, his eyes wide open. If there was even the slightest hint of something amiss, he would instantly fly over there, draw his sword, and strike. No demon could survive the sweeping strike of his sword energy.

At first, he might exchange a few words with the ghosts and spirits he encountered, but later Ji Yuan simply ignored them completely. It wasn’t that he was aloof, but rather that he didn’t have time to chat with them.

After tossing and turning for half the night, a flash of divine light appeared in the sky. Ji Yuan arrived in Dahe County this time and saw that the county was also plagued by evil spirits. Ignoring the gaze of the underworld gods and ghosts, he directly drew his sword and slashed.

“Clang—”

The clear ringing of a sword sounded out. Ji Yuan swung his sword a few times. All the ghosts, gods, and plague demons only felt a blinding white light before they sensed a piercing sharpness. Before long, it was all over.

The constant howling and wailing of the plague demons also disappeared.

This region had been on the verge of being overrun by the plague demon’s rampage, but now the crisis was almost over. Ji Yuan stood on the cloud and looked around. With his spiritual eyes, he could hardly see any place left where the plague demons’ ghostly energy gathered. He was just about to breathe a sigh of relief when he suddenly noticed a ghostly light.

Like many counties here, Dahe County did not have a City God. At this moment, there were two Nighttime Patrol Officers and a team of underworld officials currently present in the county. When they saw Ji Yuan using his sword technique to exorcise ghosts, they immediately clasped their fists and bowed.

“Thank you, Immortal Master. We are… uh…”

The Nighttime Patrol didn’t continue; Ji Yuan had already vanished before their eyes, stepping on clouds and turning into light, leaving no words behind.

The last glimmer of light in Ji Yuan’s eyes was in the direction of Maotan Village. Riding the clouds, he quickly approached Maotan Village and even heard shouts of battle. Previously, the battles between the plague demons and the underworld ghosts and gods were usually quiet, with most of the noise being from the plague ghosts.

‘Military spirits? No, not entirely!’

Upon approaching Maotan Village, he discovered that this small village was surprisingly free of plague demons; they had all been kept outside the village, and a force of several dozen armed ghosts were fighting against them.

Although the plague demons here were insignificant compared to those of the previous towns, this scene was still somewhat unbelievable, especially since Ji Yuan even saw a ghost formation derived from battle formation techniques.

“Kill! Brothers and sisters, don’t be afraid! We’ve already died once, what’s there to fear!”

“Kill…”

“Archers in the rear, once you’ve run out of arrows, throw down your bows and charge with your swords!”

Despite the presence of male and female ghosts in the rear ranks, the spirits, which should have been shrouded in a gloomy aura, they displayed an indomitable spirit upon receiving the military command.

“Kill…”

The archers threw down their bows, drew their swords, or picked up their spears from the ground, and charged toward the already precarious front.

The local Land God, who was throwing out spiritual techniques to assist, was extremely anxious. Although there was no longer a continuous stream of plague demons coming, there still seemed to be hundreds of them outside the village.

Even with these remaining plague demons, the surviving spirits of the cemetery were likely to be overwhelmed, and the Land God himself was nearly exhausted.

“Shiiing…”

The sound of a sword echoed across the land, followed by a flash of sword light. Many ghosts instinctively squinted. When they could see their surroundings again, the plague demons had disappeared, leaving only wisps of eerie light dissipating.

The Land God stared blankly at the village entrance, momentarily confused. Then, as if sensing something, he suddenly looked up and saw a white-robed immortal holding a sword standing atop a radiant cloud in the sky.

T/n: “using a cannon to kill a mosquito” is my new favorite phrase

Chapter 445: Virtue of the Living and the Dead

After drawing his sword, Ji Yuan looked curiously at the ghosts holding flags and weapons. There were men and women, young and old, and they did not seem like true military spirits at all, but their performance just now was quite remarkable.

The local Land God below was stunned for a moment, then realized what was happening and hurriedly bowed to Ji Yuan.

“Thank you for your help, Immortal Master. Otherwise, Maotan Village would surely have been overrun by the plague demons. At this moment, there must also be plague demons causing trouble in the county as well. If you have the time, Immortal Master, please lend a helping hand!”

Ji Yuan looked back in the direction he had come from, and replied to the Land God.

“There are no more plague demons in the surrounding counties. You should remain vigilant for now. Everything should be fine before dawn.”

Leaving these words behind, Ji Yuan flashed away into the distant sky once more. Now was not the time for idle talk; he would come back to this village only after he was certain that all the plague demons had been eliminated.

Once Ji Yuan left, the local Land God murmured with a look of relief on his face.

“Thank goodness…”

However, looking back at the battlefield in front of the village, the ghosts of the graveyard had suffered heavy losses, going from over a hundred at the beginning to less than half of that number now. Many of them were wandering aimlessly on the battlefield, but most were converging on a location to the side.

He gazed in the direction where the ghosts were gathering. Already, over a dozen ghosts had gathered there. The local Land God felt something was wrong and hurried over. When he grew closer, he saw that the leader of the armored soldiers was present, but he was lying down.

At this moment, the warrior’s soul was weak and semi-transparent, and was being supported by another armored warrior.

“The Land God is here! The Land God is here!”

The ghosts beside him moved aside, allowing the Land God to approach.

“Lord Land God, can you save him?”

“Yes, save the general!”

“Yes, Land God, please save the general!”

These ghosts knew that the soldier must not have held a high position in the army when he was alive, but at this moment they all called him general and begged the local Land God to save him.

“Gather around, everyone. Ghosts are yin in nature. Your gathering will benefit him. I’ll try to see if I can save him!”

At the Land God’s command, all the ghosts gathered around, forming several layers around him.

At the center, the diminutive Land God waved his cane, gathering spiritual energy with his own power, and then slowly approached the armored ghost, gently touching his forehead.

Streams of spiritual energy flowed into the ghost’s body, making the armored warrior’s form a little clearer, but it was remained translucent, as if it could dissipate at any moment.

“No, he’s too badly injured. His ghostly body is unstable. Right now, the yin energy is strong, which is good, but once dawn breaks, the sun’s energy will be at its peak. Even if he hides in his grave, it’s hard to say whether he can withstand it. And even if he can survive a day, it won’t last long. Sigh…”

The Land God’s words silenced all the ghosts.

“If only that immortal had intervened just now, but alas, he’s already gone!”

As the immortal mentioned by the local deity, Ji Yuan dared not let his guard down. He high into the sky and used his supernatural eyes to investigate. His vision was extremely sharp when it came to such peculiar evil creatures.

After about half an hour, Ji Yuan could no longer see any more plague demons, and Chang Yi had already met back up with him in the sky somewhere above Dahe County.

“Mr. Ji, strictly speaking, these things that have spawned from the earth’s poison cannot even be considered demons; calling them evil spirits would be more appropriate. Although the plague demons have been eliminated, the plague has already spread. It is a great calamity for mortals!”

Ji Yuan looked around at the land. This plague spread particularly rapidly under the influence of the plague demons. The affected area was comparable to the entirety of Ji Province. Although there were significantly fewer people affected than the population of Ji Province, due to the lower population density, it was still quite a significant number.

“There are so many infected!”

Although cultivators were named immortals by some mortals, ghosts, gods, and even demons, even the most powerful cultivators had limited abilities. At least, they could not truly bring the dead back to life, and when they could it was only under special circumstances.

From what information they had briefly gathered tonight, this plague had lasted for over half a month. moreover, due to the plague demons, the disease was extremely severe. It was manageable during the day, but at night the plague demons would wreak havoc and drain away the yang energy, so countless people had died.

Ji Yuan and Chang Yi could exterminate the plague demons and even gather a spiritual wind in the process to blow away the remaining poisonous energy left behind by the plague demons. However, they could not directly cure countless people immediately, nor could they bring the dead back to life. At most, they could let the spiritual wind blow over those currently ailing now, making them feel better and increasing the probability of recovery.

However, people’s physical condition varied, and the scope was too vast and the number too large. Ji Yuan and Chang Yi could only achieve this much even after expending a great deal of spiritual power. In the future, many more would surely die from the disease, and there would be no shortage of infected. In fact, if some of those infected escaped from this place and allowed the disease to bloom and spread again elsewhere, then even a miracle wouldn’t save them.

Seeing that Ji Yuan was frowning, Chang Yi thought for a moment and then spoke his mind.

“The country of Yuanzhao is in utter chaos. Its people are suffering, and there are bones scattered everywhere. Famine is rampant. This calamity was destined long ago, only its form has changed. Now the plague is sweeping across the land, and the state of disaster relief is certainly worrying. Its fate is sealed, and the country is doomed!”

“Yes, this country is like a dilapidated house, already plagued by war and bandits. This plague has caused even greater damage to the people and even to the underworld in large regions. This house is probably going to collapse…”

Chang Yi nodded and spoke to Ji Yuan.

“Sir, we’ve found the remnants of the earth’s evil energy. Shall we search for those demons again?”

Ji Yuan shook his head. Those demons would likely have all fled far away. Putting himself in their shoes, if he had witnessed the Heaven-Rending Sword, he would have run as far away as possible as well.1

“No need for now. Hmm, come with me somewhere.”

With that, Ji Yuan rode the clouds and flew towards Maotan Village.

There were bound to be many ghosts and spirits trying to approach Ji Yuan and Chang Yi in this large area, but precisely because there were so many, Ji Yuan avoided them as much as possible. In comparison, he was more curious about the group of ghosts fighting with weapons outside that small village.

Outside Maotan Village, the ghosts of the cemetery had not yet dissipated. After all, it was unclear whether the plague demons would return. They had to wait until dawn. But the plague demons did not return; instead, a white cloud drifted in.

The Land God and the ghosts saw two immortals were standing on the clouds from afar, one of whom was the one who had previously slain the plague demons with his sword.

“The Land God of Maotan Village, along with the ghosts of the cemetery,2 pay homage to the two immortal masters!”

Having failed in their previous etiquette, this time the Land God led the ghosts in a deep bow before the white cloud had completely landed. By the time they looked up, Ji Yuan and Chang Yi had already landed on the ground.

Upon hearing the Land God’s words, Ji Yuan repeated his question with slight surprise.

“A cemetery?”

He had previously thought that these ghosts, if not military spirits, might be the deceased ancestors of the villagers, but he was puzzled by the large number of young individuals among them. Now he understood that they were all ghosts from a cemetery.

The Land God quickly replied.

“Indeed, the ghosts fighting tonight are the remains of those the people of Maotan Village had found by the roadside and buried in the cemetery for many years. To repay this kindness, the spirits of the cemetery fought desperately to block the plague demons for the people of Maotan Village!”

As he spoke, the Land God pointed with a hint of shrewdness to the area where the ghosts were gathered.

“The armored ghost general who was leading the charge is now severely injured and is on the verge of having his soul scattered!”

“Is that so?”

Ji Yuan frowned and quickly walked towards the group of ghosts, while the ghosts around him subconsciously gave way.

When Ji Yuan arrived at the location where the ghosts had gathered, he saw a spirit in armor lying there, its body ethereal and its yin energy constantly dissipating.

“Mr. Ji, I, Chang, possess Yin Water Essence, which should be able to stabilize the ghost’s dissipation!”

Without hesitation, Chang Yi flicked his sleeve and revealed a slender, white bottle in his hand. He then approached the soul of the armored warrior and tilted the bottle slightly, revealing a drop of water with a faint luster.

“Drip… drip…”

Water droplets fell onto the armored soldier’s as if falling into water, creating ripples in his soul.

As the drop of water merged into his body, the armored ghost’s form solidified at a speed visible to the naked eye.

“That’s great, that’s great!”

“The general is saved…”

Some of the ghosts whispered among themselves, unable to hide their excitement. Although their time together was short, the armored warrior had freed them from their aimless daze, making their afterlives as ghosts more meaningful, and making them feel more human than when they were alive.

In reality, many of the ghosts in the cemetery were in a dazed and numb state, barely reacting to their surroundings. However, with the armored soldier’s encouragement, a spiritual resonance arose and grew stronger, allowing many many ghosts to awaken from their numb and dazed state, regain their senses, and rekindle their emotions.

This sense of resonance was the foundation of the camaraderie among the ghosts in this cemetery, and it was also a grace of rebirth that the armored warrior had bestowed upon these compatriots. All the ghosts here deeply respected him.

“The Yin Water Essence has a miraculous effect on stabilizing the soul, but his soul is too weak to wake up for the time being. Send him back to his grave to rest and recuperate, and he will surely awaken within three days.”

“Yes, yes, you all hurry and take him to the grave. There is nothing more to do here now!”

The Land God chime in after Chang Yi finished speaking, and the ghosts hurriedly bowed in thanks. Afterwards, they took the soul of the armored soldier back to the cemetery.

With the ghosts gone, only Ji Yuan and two others remained, including the local Land God, remained. The former looked at the still-peaceful Maotan Village and finally had the leisure to inquire about the cemetery.

The local Land God, of course, spoke frankly and recounted the events surrounding the establishment of the cemetery with a touch of emotion.

“In less than ten years, no fewer than two hundred corpses have been buried in the cemetery. The local underworld has limited power, and there are wandering ghosts everywhere, so they cannot manage everything. So I have just been guarding it all this time…”

The Land God spoke at length, and Ji Yuan and Chang Yi listened quietly the whole time. After listening to his words, Ji Yuan was moved.

“Such virtue is truly rare in chaotic times!”

He looked at Maotan Village and then at the cemetery, then Ji Yuan added another sentence.

“This applies to both the living and the dead!”

At this moment, the sky began to lighten, and dawn broke.

Chapter 446: Good People Naturally Have Good Fortune

This night was a long one for the local Land God and the ghosts of the cemetery, and equally so for all the people of Maotan Village.

As the morning sun shone brightly down on the earth, the people of Maotan Village gradually awoke. Liao Daqiu and his wife straightened up from the bed, feeling aches and pains in their backs.

“Ugh… Hey, honey, I didn’t sleep well at all last night, I had a nightmare in the middle of the night…”

The woman stopped mid-sentence, got out of bed, took a sip of water from the kettle, and then continued speaking.

“I dreamt that I was sleeping on a battlefield, surrounded by shouts of battle, as if two armies were fighting fiercely, but I just couldn’t get up. I wanted to open my eyes but couldn’t, my eyes were burning.”

Upon hearing his wife’s words, Liao Daqiu immediately became agitated.

“Me too! I couldn’t open my eyes no matter what I did, but I could feel the fierce fighting all around me, it was terrifying!”

“You dreamed about that too?”

The wife asked in surprise.

“Yes!”

After their initial agitation subsided, the two looked at each other in bewilderment before quickly getting dressed.

After Liao Daqiu finished tidying up and went outside, he saw that his neighbor, Old Zhang, had already rushed to his front door.

“Old Liao, Old Liao! Let me tell you, I had a dream last night that there was a war going on around our village. The sounds of battle were deafening, and I couldn’t open my eyes because I was so scared! It wasn’t just me; my wife had the same dream, and so did Old Liu’s family!”

Liao Daqiu swallowed hard and hurriedly took a few steps outside before speaking.

“Old Zhang, to be honest, my wife and I had the same dream. We couldn’t see anything but we could hear things, and it felt like there was a war going on around us. We felt like we were dead bodies… Ugh, I mean, it felt like we were sleeping very soundly!”

Old Zhang subconsciously took a few steps closer, looked at Liao Daqiu, and asked in a low voice.

“Old Liao, don’t you think… did the ghosts in those cemeteries already fight the plague last night?”

Liao Daqiu glanced in the direction of the cemetery and nodded subconsciously.

“We can’t say for sure!”

Then the two vaguely heard the sounds in the village getting louder and louder. The villagers, now all awake, were telling each other about the terrible dreams they had had the night before. As they talked, they realized that, except for a few people who had slept very soundly, most people had experienced similar dreams.

Some people could only hear the voices but not see the people, and knew they were on the battlefield even though they couldn’t open their eyes. Others could open their eyes a crack, though not enough to see anything aside from an eerie green light and a large number of chaotic silhouettes charging and fighting amidst the blinding footsteps.

Nobody here was stupid. This situation didn’t need to be explained too clearly; everyone could connect it to the battle flags and weapons burned for the spirits of the cemetery a while ago, and the dreams the ghosts of the cemetery and the local Land God had respectively entrusted to Liao Daqiu and the old village head.

Many villagers in Maotan didn’t even bother with breakfast. They wandered around the village several times, but found no trace of a battlefield. However, they unexpectedly encountered two outsiders.

When Liao Daqiu and the old village chief led seven or eight men from the village to the cemetery, they saw two men, one in a white robe and the other in a blue robe, standing outside looking at the cemetery. Hearing footsteps, they turned around to look at the villagers of Maotan Village, but their faces showed no surprise.

“Greetings, everyone. My name is Ji Yuan!”

“I am Chang Yi!”

Ji Yuan bowed slightly to the villagers, and Chang Yi naturally did the same.

These two men were clearly learned, and their manners were impeccable. The people of Maotan Village, of course, did not dare to be negligent and, led by the old village head, quickly returned the greeting.

“May I ask where you two come from, and what brings you to Maotan Village?”

Ji Yuan smiled.

“We’ve come from quite far away. We heard that someone here was building a public cemetery for the remains left by the roadside, so we came here specifically to see who started it.”

Everyone in Maotan Village looked at Liao Daqiu, who hesitated for a moment before stepping forward to speak.

“Well, I did initiate the cemetery project, but it was also a project that our village accomplished together. Without the help of the villagers over the years, how could I have built such a cemetery? Even the corpses are not something one person can easily move alone.”

Ji Yuan nodded.

“Indeed, you are all very righteous!”

“Haha, I dare not accept the compliment!”

“No way, we’re just doing this to accumulate good karma!”

“Yes, yes!”

The villagers felt a little embarrassed by such simple praise, but they were happy inside. These two people looked very learned, and in their hearts, the words of learned people carried a different weight.

“By the way, since you two have come from afar, it’s not very good for you to come to Dahe County at this time. You don’t know…”

Liao Daqiu glanced at the cemetery again before speaking to Ji Yuan and Chang Yi.

“Recently, it seems there’s been an epidemic in our county and the neighboring counties. This is no small matter; it can be very dangerous if you grow sick. Why did you come here at this time?”

Chang Yi let out a slight breath, shook his head slightly, and then spoke.

“Yes, there’s a plague, but not in just Dahe County and the neighboring counties you mentioned. If tallied up carefully, in this region stricken by plague, including counties and towns, there are probably close to several hundred causalties…”

“Ah!?”

“Several hundred?”

“Good heavens!”

“Is it all plague?”

“Is…is this even possible?”

The people of Maotan Village were clearly shocked by the number Chang Yi mentioned. Many of them had rarely left their own Dahe County, and the farthest they had ever gone was probably the neighboring county. Although this number included both counties and towns, just thinking about it, they could tell that it covered a very large area.

While the villagers were in astonishment, Ji Yuan looked at Liao Daqiu and observed that his aura rose in various colors, and his vitality and energy were quite strong. Although the signs of good fortune weren’t obvious, his fortune was still much better than the people around him.

“It’s so early in the morning, gentlemen. You probably haven’t had breakfast yet. Although our village is poor, we are hospitable. Why don’t you come into the village and have breakfast together?”

“Yes, yes, come and rest in our village!”

“Yes, yes, tell us about what’s happening outside. How is the plague?”

“Yes, tell us about what’s happening outside!”

Everyone immediately agreed with the old village head’s suggestion. Ji Yuan and Chang Yi exchanged a glance, and the former nodded his thanks.

“Thank you all for your kindness. In that case, we shall be disturbing you.”

“No disturbance, no disturbance at all. Gentlemen, please follow us!”

The group had searched inside and outside the village but found no signs of battle. Since they couldn’t keep searching, they welcomed Ji Yuan and Chang Yi into the village.

On the way, some people couldn’t help but tell Ji Yuan and Chang Yi about the ghosts of the cemetery appearing in their dreams, and about how almost everyone in the village had the same dream that previous night. They wanted to ask the two scholars to interpret it for them.

Ji Yuan and Chang Yi pretended to ponder it for a while, and then told the villagers directly that their visions were likely true. It was a case of good deeds being rewarded in the present, and past good deeds averting disaster. This made the people of Maotan Village very happy.

About fifteen minutes later, in the Liao family’s courtyard, Ji Yuan and Chang Yi each held a large flower-patterned bowl and sat close together on small stools.

The bowls were full of rice porridge, topped with a lot of pickled vegetables. It was the Liao family’s breakfast that they had offered the two of them. Liao Daqiu also sat eating opposite them, while his wife and children sat on the kitchen threshold.

Ji Yuan used chopsticks to pick up the pickled vegetables and skillfully scraped the porridge to eat, enjoying it immensely. This reminded him of the first time he left Ning’an County and ate porridge on a small boat on the river.

Chang Yi held the bowl with a curious expression, glancing at Ji Yuan and then back at his bowl. He tried the pickled vegetables with porridge cautiously and with great interest. He had lived on Xianxia Island for as long as he could remember. Although he was not completely ignorant of worldly customs, this was the first time he had ever eaten porridge with pickled vegetables in an ordinary household, especially since most of the porridge had been made from the previous day’s leftover rice.

“Hiss… slurp…”

Seeing Ji Yuan drinking porridge put Old Liao at ease. He had been worried that he might not be able to host them properly and had wanted to boil two eggs, but they had previously slaughtered their old hen for the feast, and there were no eggs left. Most of the neighbors were in the same situation.

After drinking more than half a bowl of hot rice porridge, Ji Yuan slowed down and started chatting with Liao Daqiu, whom he had become much more familiar with.

“By the way, I heard that Brother Liao has a son serving in the army. You and your wife worry about him often. If it’s convenient, could you tell me about him?”

Ji Yuan’s sudden mention of this matter surprised Liao Daqiu.

“Uh, Mr. Ji, who told you this?”

Liao Daqiu remembered that these two gentlemen didn’t know anyone in the village when they arrived, and on the way here, they mostly talked about the cemetery and the villagers’ dreams the previous night. How did they suddenly hear about his eldest son joining the army?

“Haha, it was the local Land God who appeared to me in a dream!”

Ji Yuan said with a smile.

‘Oh my!’

Hearing Ji Yuan’s words, a short, cane-wielding figure in a corner outside Liao Daqiu’s courtyard couldn’t help but smile knowingly, but dared not get too close.

The villagers of Maotan Village had recently experienced several instances of such dreams, and with Ji Yuan’s explanation, Liao Daqiu immediately believed it to some extent. However, there was nothing wrong with talking about it; it just stirred up some sadness.

“Sigh… My eldest son, Liao Zhengbao, has been away on the front lines for nine years, and there has been no news of him since. Several soldiers from the same draft have returned, but they all say they don’t know what happened to him. Sigh… I hope Abao1 is still alive…”

Liao Daqiu himself didn’t have much confidence as he spoke, and the woman on the other side sighed silently.

“Well, I have some basic knowledge of divination. If you don’t mind, Brother Liao, why don’t you tell me your son’s birth date and the date he went to war? I’ll be able to tell his fortune.”

The Liao family had gotten their eldest son’s fortune told countless times, and almost every time it was a matter of unpredictable destiny, or nonsense like “if he survives this calamity, he will become rich and powerful.” But out of goodwill, they nodded in agreement.

“That’s not a problem. My son was born in…”

As Liao Daqiu spoke, Ji Yuan put down his chopsticks and secretly calculated with his hands in his sleeves, Chang Yi beside him narrowed his eyes and concealed his gaze, clearly also performing a divination.

Seeing this scene from afar, even though the local Land God couldn’t tell whether the two immortals were performing a technique, or even detect any traces of spiritual energy. However, asking about Liao Zhengbao’s birth date and time was definitely an attempt to predict his fortune.

‘I never imagined that the Liao family would possess such good fortune that two immortals would divine for them together. Based on this alone, the Liao family members will be of a higher rank than ordinary ghosts in the underworld after they die!’

On the other side, as soon as Liao Daqiu finished speaking, Ji Yuan and Chang Yi stopped their divination, exchanged a glance, and nodded slightly.

“Mr. Ji, he is indeed still alive!”

“That’s right, still alive!”

Liao Daqiu and the woman were slightly taken aback upon hearing this, and their hands holding the porridge bowls trembled slightly. The two gentlemen used a definite statement, instead of the vague pronouncements of the previous fortune tellers.
